### 405 (§370): Saddhādito ṇa (`ṇa` after `saddhā` and the like: "having faith") :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§370 (405): Saddhāditoṇa" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m370" description="`ṇa` after `saddhā` and the like: 'having faith'" />
:::

> Saddhā paññā iccevamādito ṇa ppaccayo hoti ‘‘tadassatthi’’iccetasmiṃ atthe. Saddho puriso, saddhā kaññā, saddhaṃ kulaṃ. Evaṃ pañño, amaccharo, tathā buddhaṃ, buddhi assa atthīti buddho iccādi.

After `saddhā` ("faith"), `paññā` ("wisdom") and the like the suffix `ṇa` is used in the sense "he has that". `saddho puriso` ("a faithful man"), `saddhā kaññā` ("a faithful girl"), `saddhaṃ kulaṃ` ("a faithful family"). In the same way `pañño` ("wise"), `amaccharo` ("unselfish"); so too `buddhaṃ` ("having understanding", neuter), and `buddhi assa atthi` ("he has understanding"): `buddho` ("having understanding"); and so on.

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | saddhā + si (①⨀) + ṇa           | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | saddhā + ~~si~~ + ṇa            | 332 (§317) |
| →   | saddhā + (~~ṇ~~)a               | 363 (§396) |
| →   | saddh + (~~ā~~ + a)             | 67 (§83) |
| →   | saddha + si                     | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | saddha + (si→o)                 | 66 (§104) |
| →   | **saddho**                      |      |

|     | buddhi + si (①⨀) + ṇa           | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | buddhi + ~~si~~ + ṇa            | 332 (§317) |
| →   | buddhi + (~~ṇ~~)a               | 363 (§396) |
| →   | buddh + (~~i~~ + a)             | 67 (§83) |
| →   | buddha + si                     | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | buddha + (si→o)                 | 66 (§104) |
| →   | **buddho**                      |      |

:::

---

> _Assatthitaddhitaṃ._

[Thus ends] the `assatthitaddhita`, the possessive suffixes.

---

## Saṅkhyātaddhita (Numeral suffixes)

> ‘‘Pañcannaṃ pūraṇo’’ti viggahe –

In the analysis `pañcannaṃ pūraṇo` ("the completer of five") —

---

<a name="r406"></a>

### 406 (§373): Saṅkhyāpūraṇe mo (`ma` in the sense of completing a number: the ordinal) :badge[utsarga]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§373 (406): Saṅkhyāpūraṇe mo" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m373" description="`ma` in the sense of completing a number: the ordinal" />
:::

> Pūrayati saṅkhyā anenāti pūraṇo, saṅkhyāya pūraṇo saṅkhyāpūraṇo, tasmiṃ saṅkhyāpūraṇatthe chaṭṭhiyantato ma ppaccayo hoti. Pañcamo, pañcannaṃ pūraṇī pañcamī, ‘‘nadādito vā ī’’ti īpaccayo. ‘‘Itthiyamato āpaccayo’’ti ā paccayo, pañcamā vīriyapāramī, pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ. Evaṃ sattamo, sattamī, sattamā, sattamaṃ, aṭṭhamo, aṭṭhamī, aṭṭhamā, aṭṭhamaṃ, navamo, navamī, navamā, navamaṃ, dasamo, dasamī, dasamā, dasamaṃ iccādi.

A number (`saṅkhyā`) is completed (`pūrayati`) by it: hence a completer (`pūraṇa`); and the completer of a number is a `saṅkhyāpūraṇa` (an ordinal). In that sense of completing a number, after a word in the sixth form ⑥ the suffix `ma` is used: `pañcamo` ("the fifth"); `pañcannaṃ pūraṇī` ("the completer of five", feminine): `pañcamī`, with the suffix `ī` by [§238 (187): Nadādito vā ī](/kaccayana/2-namakappa/4-catutthakanda#m238); with the suffix `ā` by [§237 (176): Itthīyamato āpaccayo](/kaccayana/2-namakappa/4-catutthakanda#m237): `pañcamā vīriyapāramī` ("the fifth perfection, energy"); `pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ` ("the fifth `jhāna`"). In the same way `sattamo`, `sattamī`, `sattamā`, `sattamaṃ` ("the seventh"); `aṭṭhamo`, `aṭṭhamī`, `aṭṭhamā`, `aṭṭhamaṃ` ("the eighth"); `navamo`, `navamī`, `navamā`, `navamaṃ` ("the ninth"); `dasamo`, `dasamī`, `dasamā`, `dasamaṃ` ("the tenth"); and so on.

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | pañca + naṃ (⑥⨂) + ma           | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | pañca + ~~naṃ~~ + ma            | 332 (§317) |
| →   | pañcama + si                    | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | pañcama + (si→o)                | 66 (§104) |
| →   | **pañcamo**                     |      |

|     | pañcama + ī (🚺)                | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | pañcam + (~~a~~ + ī)            | 187 (§238), 67 (§83) |
| →   | pañcamī + si                    | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | pañcamī + ~~si~~                | 74 (§220) |
| →   | **pañcamī**                     |      |

|     | pañcama + ā (🚺)                | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | pañcam + (~~a~~ + ā)            | 176 (§237), 67 (§83) |
| →   | pañcamā + si                    | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | pañcamā + ~~si~~                | 74 (§220) |
| →   | **pañcamā**                     |      |

|     | pañcama + si (🚻①⨀)             | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | pañcama + (si→aṃ)               | 195 (§219) |
| →   | **pañcamaṃ**                    |      |

:::

> ‘‘Saṅkhyāpūraṇe’’ti adhikāro.

“`saṅkhyāpūraṇe`” ("in the sense of completing a number") is a governing rule ([`adhikāra`](/introduction#types-of-suttas)).

---

<a name="r407"></a>

### 407 (§384): Catucchehi thaṭhā (`tha`, `ṭha` after `catu`, `cha`: `catuttho`, `chaṭṭho`) :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§384 (407): Catucche hi tha ṭhā" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m384" description="`tha`, `ṭha` after `catu`, `cha`: `catuttho`, `chaṭṭho`" />
:::

> Catucha iccetehi thaṭha iccete paccayā honti saṅkhyāpūraṇatthe. Catunnaṃ pūraṇo catuttho, dvittaṃ, catutthī, catutthā, catutthaṃ, channaṃ pūraṇo chaṭṭho, chaṭṭhī, chaṭṭhā, chaṭṭhaṃ, chaṭṭho eva chaṭṭhamo.

After `catu` ("four") and `cha` ("six") the suffixes `tha` and `ṭha` are used in the sense of completing a number. `catunnaṃ pūraṇo` ("the completer of four"): `catuttho` ("the fourth"), with doubling; `catutthī`, `catutthā`, `catutthaṃ`. `channaṃ pūraṇo` ("the completer of six"): `chaṭṭho` ("the sixth"); `chaṭṭhī`, `chaṭṭhā`, `chaṭṭhaṃ`. `chaṭṭhamo` is simply `chaṭṭho`.

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | catu + naṃ (⑥⨂) + tha           | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | catu + ~~naṃ~~ + tha            | 332 (§317) |
| →   | catu + (th→tth) + a             | 42 (§29) |
| →   | catuttha + si                   | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | catuttha + (si→o)               | 66 (§104) |
| →   | **catuttho**                    |      |

|     | cha + naṃ (⑥⨂) + ṭha            | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | cha + ~~naṃ~~ + ṭha             | 332 (§317) |
| →   | cha + (ṭ)ṭha                    | 42 (§29) |
| →   | chaṭṭha + si                    | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | chaṭṭha + (si→o)                | 66 (§104) |
| →   | **chaṭṭho**                     |      |

|     | chaṭṭha + ma                    | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | chaṭṭhama + si                  | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | chaṭṭhama + (si→o)              | 66 (§104) |
| →   | **chaṭṭhamo**                   |      |

:::

> Chāhaṃ, chaḷāyatanaṃ iccatra –

In `chāhaṃ` ("six days") and `chaḷāyatanaṃ` ("the six sense bases"), here —

---

<a name="r408"></a>

### 408 (§374): Sa chassa vā (Optionally `sa` for `cha` [in the ordinal]: `saṭṭho`) :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§374 (408): Sa chassavā" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m374" description="Optionally `sa` for `cha` [in the ordinal]: `saṭṭho`" />
:::

> Cha ssa sa kārādeso hoti vā saṅkhyāne. Chāhamassa jīvitaṃ sāhaṃ, chāhaṃ vā, saḷāyatanaṃ.

In a numeral, `cha` is optionally replaced by the letter `sa`. `chāhaṃ assa jīvitaṃ` ("his life is six days"): `sāhaṃ`, or `chāhaṃ` ("six days"); `saḷāyatanaṃ` ("the six sense bases").

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | cha + aha                       | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ch + (~~a~~ + a)ha              | 13 (§12) |
| →   | ch + (a→ā)ha                    | 17 (§15) |
| →   | (cha→sa) + āha                  | 408 (§374) (optional) |
| →   | sāha + si                       | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | sāha + (si→aṃ)                  | 195 (§219) |
| →   | **sāhaṃ**, **chāhaṃ**           |      |

|     | cha + āyatana                   | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | cha + (ḷ) + āyatana             | 34 (§35) |
| →   | (cha→sa) + ḷāyatana             | 408 (§374) (optional) |
| →   | saḷāyatana + si                 | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | saḷāyatana + (si→aṃ)            | 195 (§219) |
| →   | **saḷāyatanaṃ**, **chaḷāyatanaṃ** |      |

:::

:::tip[Examples]

> `nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapaccayā phasso` \
> [(3V 1.1: Bodhikathā)](https://tipitaka2500.github.io/tipitaka/3V/1/1.1#3) \
🚹⑤⨀(nāmarūpa + paccaya) 🚻①⨀(cha + āyatana) 🚹⑤⨀(saḷāyatana + paccaya) 🚹①⨀(phassa) \
with name-and-form as condition | the six sense bases | with the six sense bases as condition | contact

:::

> ‘‘Dvinnaṃ pūraṇo’’ti viggahe –

In the analysis `dvinnaṃ pūraṇo` ("the completer of two") —

---

<a name="r409"></a>

### 409 (§385): Dvitīhi tiyo (`tiya` after `dvi`, `ti`: `dutiyo`, `tatiyo`) :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§385 (409): Dvitīhi tiyo" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m385" description="`tiya` after `dvi`, `ti`: `dutiyo`, `tatiyo`" />
:::

> Dviti iccetehi tiya ppaccayo hoti saṅkhyāpūraṇatthe. Vipariṇāmena ‘‘dvi tiṇṇa’’nti vattamāne –

After `dvi` ("two") and `ti` ("three") the suffix `tiya` is used in the sense of completing a number. While “`dvitiṇṇaṃ`” ("of `dvi` and `ti`") continues, by a change of inflection (`vipariṇāma`) —

---

<a name="r410"></a>

### 410 (§386): Tiye dutāpi ca (`du`, `ta` for `dvi`, `ti` before `tiya`; `du`, `di` elsewhere too) :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§386 (410): Tiye dutāpi ca" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m386" description="`du`, `ta` for `dvi`, `ti` before `tiya`; `du`, `di` elsewhere too" />
:::

> Dviti iccetesaṃ duta iccādesā honti tiya ppaccaye pare. Dutiyo puriso, dutiyā, dutiyaṃ. Evaṃ tiṇṇaṃ pūraṇo tatiyo, tatiyā, tatiyaṃ. Api ggahaṇena aññatthāpi dvi saddassa du ādeso hoti, ca saddena di ca. Dve rattiyo durattaṃ, duvidhaṃ, duvaṅgaṃ, dirattaṃ, diguṇaṃ, digu.

`dvi` and `ti` are replaced by `du` and `ta` when the suffix `tiya` follows: `dutiyo puriso` ("the second man"), `dutiyā`, `dutiyaṃ`. In the same way `tiṇṇaṃ pūraṇo` ("the completer of three"): `tatiyo` ("the third"), `tatiyā`, `tatiyaṃ`. By the mention of `api`, the word `dvi` is replaced by `du` elsewhere too, and by the word `ca`, by `di` as well: `dve rattiyo` ("two nights"): `durattaṃ`; `duvidhaṃ` ("twofold"), `duvaṅgaṃ` ("two-membered"); `dirattaṃ` ("two nights"), `diguṇaṃ` ("double"), `digu` ("two cows", the numerical compound).

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | dvi + naṃ (⑥⨂) + tiya           | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | dvi + ~~naṃ~~ + tiya            | 332 (§317) |
| →   | (dvi→du) + tiya                 | 410 (§386) |
| →   | dutiya + si                     | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | dutiya + (si→o)                 | 66 (§104) |
| →   | **dutiyo**                      |      |

|     | ti + naṃ (⑥⨂) + tiya            | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ti + ~~naṃ~~ + tiya             | 332 (§317) |
| →   | (ti→ta) + tiya                  | 410 (§386) |
| →   | tatiya + si                     | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | tatiya + (si→o)                 | 66 (§104) |
| →   | **tatiyo**                      |      |

|     | dvi + ratti (digu)              | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | (dvi→du) + ratti                | 410 (§386) (or `di`, by its `ca`) |
| →   | duratt + (~~i~~ + a)            | 350 (§337), 67 (§83) |
| →   | duratta + si                    | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | duratta + (si→aṃ)               | 195 (§219) |
| →   | **durattaṃ**, **dirattaṃ**      |      |

:::

---

<a name="r411"></a>

### 411 (§387): Tesamaḍḍhūpapadena aḍḍhuḍḍhadivaḍḍhadiyaḍḍhaḍḍhatiyā (with `aḍḍha` before them: `aḍḍhuḍḍha`, `divaḍḍha`, `diyaḍḍha`, `aḍḍhatiya`) :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§387 (411): Tesamaḍḍhūpapadena aḍḍhuḍḍha divaḍḍha diyaḍḍhaḍḍhatiyā" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m387" description="with `aḍḍha` before them: `aḍḍhuḍḍha`, `divaḍḍha`, `diyaḍḍha`, `aḍḍhatiya`" />
:::

> Tesaṃ catutthadutiyatatiyānaṃ aḍḍhūpapadānaṃ aḍḍhūpapadena saha aḍḍhuḍḍhadivaḍḍhadiyaḍḍhaaḍḍhatiyā desā honti.

Of these, `catuttha` ("fourth"), `dutiya` ("second") and `tatiya` ("third"), when `aḍḍha` ("half") is their accompanying word (`upapada`), are replaced, together with that preceding word `aḍḍha`, by `aḍḍhuḍḍha`, `divaḍḍha`, `diyaḍḍha` and `aḍḍhatiya`.

> Ettha ca –

And here —

> Aḍḍhūpapadapādāna-sāmatthā aḍḍhapubbakā; \
> Tesaṃ saddena gayhante, catutthadutiyādayo.

By the force of the mention of the preceding word `aḍḍha`, \
`catuttha`, `dutiya` and the rest, with `aḍḍha` before them, are taken in by the word `tesaṃ`.

> Aḍḍhena catuttho aḍḍhuḍḍho, aḍḍhena dutiyo divaḍḍho, diyaḍḍho, aḍḍhena tatiyo aḍḍhatiyo.

`aḍḍhena catuttho` ("the fourth by a half"): `aḍḍhuḍḍho` ("three and a half"); `aḍḍhena dutiyo` ("the second by a half"): `divaḍḍho`, `diyaḍḍho` ("one and a half"); `aḍḍhena tatiyo` ("the third by a half"): `aḍḍhatiyo` ("two and a half").

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | aḍḍha + catuttha                | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | (aḍḍha catuttha → aḍḍhuḍḍha)    | 411 (§387) |
| →   | aḍḍhuḍḍha + si                  | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | aḍḍhuḍḍha + (si→o)              | 66 (§104) |
| →   | **aḍḍhuḍḍho**                   |      |

|     | aḍḍha + dutiya                  | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | (aḍḍha dutiya → divaḍḍha, diyaḍḍha) | 411 (§387) |
| →   | divaḍḍha + si                   | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | divaḍḍha + (si→o)               | 66 (§104) |
| →   | **divaḍḍho**, **diyaḍḍho**      |      |

|     | aḍḍha + tatiya                  | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | (aḍḍha tatiya → aḍḍhatiya)      | 411 (§387) |
| →   | aḍḍhatiya + si                  | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | aḍḍhatiya + (si→o)              | 66 (§104) |
| →   | **aḍḍhatiyo**                   |      |

:::

> ‘‘Ekañca dasa cā’’ti atthe dvandasamāse, ‘‘ekena adhikā dasā’’ti atthe tappurisasamāse vā kate ‘‘saṅkhyāne’’ti vattamāne ‘‘dvekaṭṭhānamākāro vā’’ti ā ttaṃ. Vavatthitavibhāsatthoyaṃ vā saddo.

When a `dvanda` compound has been made in the sense `ekaṃ ca dasa ca` ("one and ten"), or a `tappurisa` compound in the sense `ekena adhikā dasa` ("ten exceeded by one"), then, while “`saṅkhyāne`” ("in a numeral") continues, the change to `ā` is made by [§383 (253): Dvekaṭṭhānamākāro vā](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m383). This word `vā` has the sense of a restricted option (`vavatthitavibhāsā`).

> Tena cettha –

And therefore here —

> Dvekaṭṭhānaṃ dase niccaṃ, dvissā’navutiyā navā; \
> Itaresa’masantañca, āttaṃ dīpeti vā suti.

Constant for `dvi`, `eka` and `aṭṭha` before `dasa`, in some places (`navā`) for `dvi` as far as `navuti`, \
and absent for the rest: such is the change to `ā` that the word `vā` shows.

> ‘‘Ekādito dasara saṅkhyāne’’ti rattaṃ.

The change to `r` is made by [§381 (254): Ekādito dassa ra saṅkhyāne](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m381).

> Rā deso vaṇṇamattattā, vaṇṇamattappasaṅgipi; \
> Siyā dasassa dasseva, nimittāsannabhāvato.

Though the replacement `ra`, being a mere letter, could apply to any mere letter, \
let it be of the `d` of `dasa` alone, because of the nearness of the condition (`nimitta`).

> Tato bahuvacanaṃ yo, ‘‘pañcādīnamakāro’’ti savibhattissa antassa a ttaṃ. Ekārasa, ekādasa, liṅgattayepi samānaṃ.

Then comes the plural `yo`, and by [§134 (251): Pañcādīnamakāro](/kaccayana/2-namakappa/2-dutiyakanda#m134) the final together with its inflection becomes `a`: `ekārasa`, `ekādasa` ("eleven"), the same in all three genders too.

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | eka + dasa (dvanda)             | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | eka + ~~si~~ + dasa + ~~yo~~    | 332 (§317) |
| →   | ek(a→ā) + dasa                  | 253 (§383) |
| →   | ekā + (d→r)asa                  | 254 (§381) (optional) |
| →   | ekārasa + yo (①⨂)               | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | ekāras(a + yo → a)              | 251 (§134) |
| →   | **ekārasa**, **ekādasa**        |      |

:::

> ‘‘Vā’’ti vattate.

“`vā`” continues.

---

<a name="r412"></a>

### 412 (§375): Ekādito dasassī (Optionally `ī` after `dasa` preceded by `eka` and the like: the feminine ordinal) :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§375 (412): Ekādito dasassī" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m375" description="Optionally `ī` after `dasa` preceded by `eka` and the like: the feminine ordinal" />
:::

:::note[Analysis]

The “`gaṇane dasassa`” quoted under `catuddasa` below opens [413 (§389): Gaṇane dasassa…](/rupasiddhi/5-taddhitakanda#r413), which is stated only after this rule; the “`saṅkhyānaṃ`” said to continue under `soḷasa` is the “`saṅkhyāne`” of [408 (§374): Sa chassa vā](/rupasiddhi/5-taddhitakanda#r408).

:::

> E kādito parassa dasa ssa ante īpaccayo hoti vā pūraṇatthe.

After `dasa` preceded by `eka` and the like, the suffix `ī` is optionally added at the end, in the sense of completing a number.

> Dasassa paccayāyogā, laddhamanteti atthato; \
> Tadantassa sabhāvena, itthiyaṃyeva sambhavo.

Since a suffix cannot be joined to `dasa`, the word `ante` ("at the end") is obtained from the sense; \
and by the nature of what ends in that `ī`, it occurs in the feminine alone.

> Ekādasannaṃ pūraṇī e kādasī, aññatra ekādasamo, ekādasamaṃ.

`ekādasannaṃ pūraṇī` ("the completer of eleven"): `ekādasī` ("the eleventh"); elsewhere `ekādasamo`, `ekādasamaṃ` ("the eleventh", masculine and neuter).

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | ekādasa + naṃ (⑥⨂) + ī          | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ekādasa + ~~naṃ~~ + ī           | 332 (§317) |
| →   | ekādas + (~~a~~ + ī)            | 412 (§375), 67 (§83) |
| →   | ekādasī + si                    | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | ekādasī + ~~si~~                | 74 (§220) |
| →   | **ekādasī**                     |      |

|     | ekādasa + naṃ (⑥⨂) + ma         | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ekādasa + ~~naṃ~~ + ma          | 332 (§317) |
| →   | ekādasama + si                  | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | ekādasama + (si→o)              | 66 (§104) |
| →   | **ekādasamo**                   |      |

:::

> Dve ca dasa ca, dvīhi vā adhikā dasāti ‘‘dvi dasa’’iccatra ‘‘vā’’ti vattate.

`dve ca dasa ca` ("two and ten"), or `dvīhi adhikā dasa` ("ten exceeded by two"): here, in `dvi` + `dasa`, “`vā`” continues.

> ‘‘Vīsati dasesu bā dvissa tū’’ti bā deso, da ssa rā deso. Bārasa, aññatra ā ttaṃ, dvādasa. Dvādasannaṃ pūraṇo bārasamo, dvādasamo, dvādasī.

By [§380 (255): Vīsati dasesu bā dvissa tu](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m380) `bā` is the replacement, and the `d` is replaced by `r`: `bārasa` ("twelve"); otherwise, with the change to `ā`, `dvādasa`. `dvādasannaṃ pūraṇo` ("the completer of twelve"): `bārasamo`, `dvādasamo` ("the twelfth"); `dvādasī`.

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | dvi + dasa (dvanda)             | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | dvi + ~~yo~~ + dasa + ~~yo~~    | 332 (§317) |
| →   | (dvi→bā) + dasa                 | 255 (§380) |
| →   | bā + (d→r)asa                   | 254 (§381) |
| →   | bāras(a + yo → a)               | 251 (§134) |
| →   | **bārasa**                      |      |

|     | dvi + dasa (dvanda)             | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | dvi + ~~yo~~ + dasa + ~~yo~~    | 332 (§317) |
| →   | dv(i→ā) + dasa                  | 253 (§383) |
| →   | dvādas(a + yo → a)              | 251 (§134) |
| →   | **dvādasa**                     |      |

:::

> Tayo ca dasa ca, tīhi vā adhikā dasāti terasa, ‘‘tesu vuddhi lopā’’dinā ti ssa te ādeso[^9] ānavutiyā, terasamo, terasī.

[^9]: CST4 prints `teādaso`; the word is `ādeso`, as in the `bādeso` and `rādeso` of the sentence before.

`tayo ca dasa ca` ("three and ten"), or `tīhi adhikā dasa` ("ten exceeded by three"): `terasa` ("thirteen"); by [§404 (370): Tesu vuddhilopāgamavikāraviparītādesā ca](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m404) `ti` is replaced by `te`, as far as `navuti`; `terasamo`, `terasī` ("the thirteenth").

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | ti + dasa (dvanda)              | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ti + ~~yo~~ + dasa + ~~yo~~     | 332 (§317) |
| →   | (ti→te) + dasa                  | 370 (§404) |
| →   | te + (d→r)asa                   | 254 (§381) |
| →   | teras(a + yo → a)               | 251 (§134) |
| →   | **terasa**                      |      |

:::

> Cattāro ca dasa ca, catūhi vā adhikā dasāti catuddasa iccatra ‘‘gaṇane dasassā’’ti ca vattamāne ‘‘catūpapadassa lopo tuttarapadādi cassa cucopi navā’’ti tu lopo, cuco ca. Cuddasa, coddasa, catuddasa. Cuddasamo, catuddasamo, catuddasī[^10], cātuddasī vā.

[^10]: CST4 prints `cakuddasī`; the form is `catuddasī`, the feminine ordinal beside `cātuddasī` with the lengthening.

`cattāro ca dasa ca` ("four and ten"), or `catūhi adhikā dasa` ("ten exceeded by four"): `catuddasa` ("fourteen"). Here, while “`gaṇane dasassa`” ([§389 (413): Gaṇane dasassa dviticatupañcachasattaaṭṭhanavakānaṃ vī ti cattāra paññā cha sattāsanavā yosu, yonañcīsamāsaṃ ṭhi ri tī tuti](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m389)) also continues, by [§390 (256): Catūpapadassa lopo tuttarapadādicassa cucopi navā](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m390) the `tu` is elided, and `cu` and `co` come in as well: `cuddasa`, `coddasa`, `catuddasa`. `cuddasamo`, `catuddasamo` ("the fourteenth"); `catuddasī`, or `cātuddasī`.

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | catu + dasa (dvanda)            | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | catu + ~~yo~~ + dasa + ~~yo~~   | 332 (§317) |
| →   | ca + (~~tu~~) + dasa            | 256 (§390) (optional) |
| →   | (ca→cu, co) + dasa              | 256 (§390) (optional) |
| →   | cu + (d)dasa                    | 40 (§28)  |
| →   | cuddas(a + yo → a)              | 251 (§134) |
| →   | **cuddasa**, **coddasa**, **catuddasa** |      |

|     | catuddasa + naṃ (⑥⨂) + ī        | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | catuddasa + ~~naṃ~~ + ī         | 332 (§317) |
| →   | catuddas + (~~a~~ + ī)          | 412 (§375), 67 (§83) |
| →   | c(a→ā)tuddasī                   | 354 (§403) (optional) |
| →   | cātuddasī + si                  | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | cātuddasī + ~~si~~              | 74 (§220) |
| →   | **cātuddasī**, **catuddasī**    |      |

:::

> Pañca ca dasa ca, pañcahi vā adhikā dasāti pañcadasa, ‘‘tesu vuddhi lopā’’dinā pañcasaddassa dasa vīsesu pannapaṇṇa ādesāpi, ‘‘aṭṭhādito cā’’ti rattaṃ. Pannarasa, pañcadasa. Pannarasamo, pañcadasamo, pannarasī, pañcadasī.

`pañca ca dasa ca` ("five and ten"), or `pañcahi adhikā dasa` ("ten exceeded by five"): `pañcadasa` ("fifteen"). By [§404 (370): Tesu vuddhilopāgamavikāraviparītādesā ca](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m404) the word `pañca` is also replaced by `panna` and `paṇṇa` before `dasa` and `vīsa`, and the change to `r` is made by [§382 (259): Aṭṭhādito ca](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m382): `pannarasa`, `pañcadasa`. `pannarasamo`, `pañcadasamo` ("the fifteenth"); `pannarasī`, `pañcadasī`.

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | pañca + dasa (dvanda)           | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | pañca + ~~yo~~ + dasa + ~~yo~~  | 332 (§317) |
| →   | (pañca→panna) + dasa            | 370 (§404) (optional) |
| →   | panna + (d→r)asa                | 259 (§382) (optional) |
| →   | pannaras(a + yo → a)            | 251 (§134) |
| →   | **pannarasa**, **pañcadasa**    |      |

:::

> ‘‘Cha ca dasa ca, chahi vā adhikā dasā’’ti samāse kate ‘‘chassā’’ti vattamāne ‘‘dase so niccañcā’’ti so, ‘‘saṅkhyānaṃ, vā’’ti ca vattate, ‘‘la darāna’’nti lattaṃ, vavatthitavibhāsatthoyaṃ vā saddo.

When the compound has been made in the sense `cha ca dasa ca` ("six and ten"), or `chahi adhikā dasa` ("ten exceeded by six"), then, while “`chassa`” ("of `cha`") continues, it is replaced by `so` by [§376 (257): Dase so niccañca](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m376); “`saṅkhyānaṃ`” and “`vā`” also continue, and the change to `l` is made by [§379 (258): La darānaṃ](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m379). This word `vā` has the sense of a restricted option (`vavatthitavibhāsā`).

> Ḷo niccaṃ soḷase dassa, cattālīse ca terase; \
> Aññatra na ca hotāyaṃ, vavatthitavibhāsato.

`ḷ` for the `d` is constant in `soḷasa`, and occurs in `cattālīsa` and `terasa`; \
elsewhere it does not occur at all, by the restricted option.

> Laḷānamaviseso kvaci, soḷasa. Teḷasa, cattālīsaṃ, cattārīsaṃ. Soḷasamo, soḷasī.

In some places there is no distinction between `l` and `ḷ`: `soḷasa` ("sixteen"); `teḷasa` ("thirteen"); `cattālīsaṃ`, `cattārīsaṃ` ("forty"). `soḷasamo`, `soḷasī` ("the sixteenth").

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | cha + dasa (dvanda)             | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | cha + ~~yo~~ + dasa + ~~yo~~    | 332 (§317) |
| →   | (cha→so) + dasa                 | 257 (§376) |
| →   | so + (d→ḷ)asa                   | 258 (§379) |
| →   | soḷas(a + yo → a)               | 251 (§134) |
| →   | **soḷasa**                      |      |

:::

> ‘‘Vā, dasara saṅkhyāne’’ti adhikicca ‘‘aṭṭhādito cā’’ti rattaṃ. Aṭṭhārasa, aṭṭhādasa, ā ttaṃ. Aṭṭhārasannaṃ pūraṇo aṭṭhārasamo, aṭṭhādasamo. Evaṃ sattarasa, sattadasa. Sattarasamo, sattadasamo.

With “`vā`” and “`dasara saṅkhyāne`” ([§381 (254): Ekādito dassa ra saṅkhyāne](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m381)) governing, the change to `r` is made by [§382 (259): Aṭṭhādito ca](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m382): `aṭṭhārasa`, `aṭṭhādasa` ("eighteen"), with the change to `ā`. `aṭṭhārasannaṃ pūraṇo` ("the completer of eighteen"): `aṭṭhārasamo`, `aṭṭhādasamo` ("the eighteenth"). In the same way `sattarasa`, `sattadasa` ("seventeen"); `sattarasamo`, `sattadasamo` ("the seventeenth").

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | aṭṭha + dasa (dvanda)           | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | aṭṭha + ~~yo~~ + dasa + ~~yo~~  | 332 (§317) |
| →   | aṭṭh(a→ā) + dasa                | 253 (§383) |
| →   | aṭṭhā + (d→r)asa                | 259 (§382) (optional) |
| →   | aṭṭhāras(a + yo → a)            | 251 (§134) |
| →   | **aṭṭhārasa**, **aṭṭhādasa**    |      |

|     | satta + dasa (dvanda)           | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | satta + ~~yo~~ + dasa + ~~yo~~  | 332 (§317) |
| →   | satta + (d→r)asa                | 259 (§382) (optional) |
| →   | sattaras(a + yo → a)            | 251 (§134) |
| →   | **sattarasa**, **sattadasa**    |      |

:::

> Aṭṭhādito ti kimatthaṃ? Catuddasa.

By saying `aṭṭhādito` ("after `aṭṭha` and the like") we exclude the change to `r` after the other numerals: `catuddasa` ("fourteen").

> Ekena ūnā vīsatīti tappuriso, ekūnavīsati. Ekūnavīsatādayo ānavutiyā e kavacanantā, itthiliṅgā ca daṭṭhabbā, te ca saṅkhyāne, saṅkhyeyye ca vattante, yadā saṅkhyāne vattante, tadā bhikkhūnamekūnavīsati tiṭṭhati, bhoti bhikkhūnamekūnavīsati tiṭṭhatu, bhikkhūnamekūnavīsatiṃ passa, bhikkhūnamekūnavīsatiyā kataṃ iccādi.

`ekena ūnā vīsati` ("twenty less by one"), a `tappurisa`: `ekūnavīsati` ("nineteen"). `ekūnavīsati` and the rest, as far as `navuti`, should be understood as singular and feminine, and they occur both in the sense of the number (`saṅkhyāna`) and in that of the thing counted (`saṅkhyeyya`). When they occur in the sense of the number: `bhikkhūnaṃ ekūnavīsati tiṭṭhati` ("a nineteen of monks stands"), `bhoti bhikkhūnaṃ ekūnavīsati tiṭṭhatu` ("O nineteen of monks, let it stand!"), `bhikkhūnaṃ ekūnavīsatiṃ passa` ("see the nineteen of monks"), `bhikkhūnaṃ ekūnavīsatiyā kataṃ` ("done by the nineteen of monks"), and so on.

> Saṅkhyeyye pana ekūnavīsati bhikkhavo tiṭṭhanti, bhonto ekūnavīsati bhikkhavo tiṭṭhatha, ekūnavīsatiṃ bhikkhū passa, ekūnavīsatiyā bhikkhūhi kataṃ iccādi. Evaṃ vīsatādīsupi yojetabbaṃ, ekūnavīsatiyā pūraṇo ekūnavīsatimo.

But in the sense of the thing counted: `ekūnavīsati bhikkhavo tiṭṭhanti` ("nineteen monks stand"), `bhonto ekūnavīsati bhikkhavo tiṭṭhatha` ("O nineteen monks, stand!"), `ekūnavīsatiṃ bhikkhū passa` ("see nineteen monks"), `ekūnavīsatiyā bhikkhūhi kataṃ` ("done by nineteen monks"), and so on. The same should be applied to `vīsati` and the rest too. `ekūnavīsatiyā pūraṇo` ("the completer of nineteen"): `ekūnavīsatimo` ("the nineteenth").

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | eka + ūna + vīsati (tappurisa)  | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | eka + ~~nā~~ + ūna + vīsati + ~~si~~ | 332 (§317) |
| →   | ek + (~~a~~ + ū)na + vīsati     | 13 (§12)  |
| →   | ekūnavīsati + si                | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | ekūnavīsati + ~~si~~            | 74 (§220) |
| →   | **ekūnavīsati**                 |      |

:::

> Dasa ca dasa cāti atthe dvandasamāsaṃ katvā ‘‘dasadasā’’ti vattabbe ‘‘sarūpānamekasesvasaki’’nti ekasese kate dasa saddato paṭhamābahuvacanaṃ yo. ‘‘Dasa yo’’itīdha –

Having made a `dvanda` compound in the sense `dasa ca dasa ca` ("ten and ten"), when `dasadasa` would have to be said, one alone is left (`ekasesa`) by [§388 (68): Sarūpānamekasesvasakiṃ](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m388), and after the word `dasa` comes the plural `yo` of the first form ①⨂. Here, in `dasa` + `yo` —

---

<a name="r413"></a>

### 413 (§389): Gaṇane dasassa dviti catu pañca cha satta aṭṭhanavakānaṃ vīti cattāra paññā cha sattāsa navā yosu yonañcī samāsaṃ ṭhiri tītuti (the tens: `vī`…`nava` for repeated `dasa`, `īsaṃ`…`uti` for its `yo`) :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§389 (413): Gaṇane dasassa dviticatupañcachasattaaṭṭhanavakānaṃ vī ti cattāra paññā cha sattāsanavā yosu, yonañcīsamāsaṃ ṭhi ri tī tuti" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m389" description="the tens: `vī`…`nava` for repeated `dasa`, `īsaṃ`…`uti` for its `yo`" />
:::

> Gaṇane dasassa sambandhīnaṃ dvika tika catukka pañcaka chakkasattaka aṭṭhaka navakānaṃ katekasesānaṃ yathākkamaṃ vīti cattāra paññā cha satta asa nava iccādesā honti yo su paresu, yonañca īsaṃ āsaṃ ṭhi riti īti uti iccete ādesā hontīti dvidasatthavācakassa dasa ssa vī ādeso hoti, yo vacanassa īsa ñca, saralopādi.

In counting, when `dasa` belongs to a set of two, three, four, five, six, seven, eight or nine and one alone has been left, it is replaced in order by `vī`, `ti`, `cattāra`, `paññā`, `cha`, `satta`, `asa` and `nava` when `yo` follows, and the `yo` is replaced by `īsaṃ`, `āsaṃ`, `ṭhi`, `ri`, `ti`, `īti` and `uti`. So the `dasa` that expresses two tens is replaced by `vī`, and the inflection `yo` by `īsaṃ`; then the elision of the vowel and the rest.

> ‘‘Saṅkhyānaṃ, vā, ante’’ti ca vattate.

“`saṅkhyānaṃ`”, “`vā`” and “`ante`” also continue.

---

<a name="r414"></a>

### 414 (§378): Ti ca (And `ti` at the end: `vīsati`, `tiṃsati`) :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§378 (414): Ti ca" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m378" description="And `ti` at the end: `vīsati`, `tiṃsati`" />
:::

:::note[Analysis]

The “`vā`” and “`saṅkhyānaṃ`” that continue into this rule are those of [408 (§374): Sa chassa vā](/rupasiddhi/5-taddhitakanda#r408), and the “`ante`” is that of [412 (§375): Ekādito dasassī](/rupasiddhi/5-taddhitakanda#r412).

:::

> Tāsaṃ saṅkhyānamante ti kārāgamo hoti vā. Vavatthitavibhāsatthoyaṃ vā saddo.

At the end of those numerals, `ti` is optionally added as an insertion ([`āgama`](/introduction#types-of-vidhi-operation)). This word `vā` has the sense of a restricted option (`vavatthitavibhāsā`).

> Vibhāsā vīsa tiṃsāna-mante hoti tiāgamo; \
> Aññattha na ca hoteva, vavatthitavibhāsato.

The insertion of `ti` is optional (`vibhāsā`) at the end of `vīsa` and `tiṃsa`; \
elsewhere it does not occur at all, by the restricted option.

> ‘‘Byañjane cā’’ti niggahītalopo, puna taddhitattā nāmabyapadese syā dyuppatti. Bhikkhūnaṃ vīsati, vīsaṃ vā, vīsati bhikkhū, vīsaṃ vā iccādi. Vīsatimo. Tathā ekavīsati kusalacittāni, ekavīsaṃ vā. Ekavīsatimo. Bāvīsati, bāvīsaṃ vā. Bāvīsatimo. Dvāvīsati, dvāvīsaṃ vā. Dvāvīsatimo. Tevīsati, tevīsaṃ vā. Tevīsatimo. Catuvīsati, catuvīsaṃ vā. Catuvīsatimo. Paṇṇavīsati, paṇṇavīsaṃ vā. Paṇṇavīsatimo. Pañcavīsati, pañcavīsaṃ vā. Pañcavīsatimo. Chabbīsati, chabbīsaṃ vā. Chabbīsatimo. Sattavīsati, sattavīsaṃ vā. Sattavīsatimo. Aṭṭhavīsati, aṭṭhavīsaṃ vā. Aṭṭhavīsatimo. Ekūnatiṃsati, ekūnatiṃsaṃvā. Ekūnatiṃsatimo.

The niggahīta is elided by [§39 (54): Byañjane ca](/kaccayana/1-sandhikappa#m39); then, since the word is a `taddhita`, it is treated as a noun again and the inflections `si` and the rest arise: `bhikkhūnaṃ vīsati` ("a twenty of monks"), or `vīsaṃ`; `vīsati bhikkhū` ("twenty monks"), or `vīsaṃ`; and so on. `vīsatimo` ("the twentieth"). Likewise `ekavīsati kusalacittāni` ("the twenty-one wholesome consciousnesses"), or `ekavīsaṃ`; `ekavīsatimo` ("the twenty-first"). `bāvīsati` ("twenty-two"), or `bāvīsaṃ`; `bāvīsatimo`. `dvāvīsati`, or `dvāvīsaṃ`; `dvāvīsatimo`. `tevīsati` ("twenty-three"), or `tevīsaṃ`; `tevīsatimo`. `catuvīsati` ("twenty-four"), or `catuvīsaṃ`; `catuvīsatimo`. `paṇṇavīsati` ("twenty-five"), or `paṇṇavīsaṃ`; `paṇṇavīsatimo`. `pañcavīsati`, or `pañcavīsaṃ`; `pañcavīsatimo`. `chabbīsati` ("twenty-six"), or `chabbīsaṃ`; `chabbīsatimo`. `sattavīsati` ("twenty-seven"), or `sattavīsaṃ`; `sattavīsatimo`. `aṭṭhavīsati` ("twenty-eight"), or `aṭṭhavīsaṃ`; `aṭṭhavīsatimo`. `ekūnatiṃsati` ("twenty-nine"), or `ekūnatiṃsaṃ`; `ekūnatiṃsatimo`.

| number | with `ti` | with `ṃ` | ordinal |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 20 | vīsati | vīsaṃ | vīsatimo |
| 21 | ekavīsati | ekavīsaṃ | ekavīsatimo |
| 22 | bāvīsati | bāvīsaṃ | bāvīsatimo |
| 22 | dvāvīsati | dvāvīsaṃ | dvāvīsatimo |
| 23 | tevīsati | tevīsaṃ | tevīsatimo |
| 24 | catuvīsati | catuvīsaṃ | catuvīsatimo |
| 25 | paṇṇavīsati | paṇṇavīsaṃ | paṇṇavīsatimo |
| 25 | pañcavīsati | pañcavīsaṃ | pañcavīsatimo |
| 26 | chabbīsati | chabbīsaṃ | chabbīsatimo |
| 27 | sattavīsati | sattavīsaṃ | sattavīsatimo |
| 28 | aṭṭhavīsati | aṭṭhavīsaṃ | aṭṭhavīsatimo |
| 29 | ekūnatiṃsati | ekūnatiṃsaṃ | ekūnatiṃsatimo |

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | vīsaṃ                           | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | vīsaṃ + (ti)                    | 414 (§378) (optional) |
| →   | vīsa + ~~ṃ~~ + ti               | 54 (§39)  |
| →   | vīsati + si                     | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | vīsati + ~~si~~                 | 74 (§220) |
| →   | **vīsati**, **vīsaṃ**           |      |

|     | dvi + vīsati                    | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | (dvi→bā) + vīsati               | 255 (§380) (optional) |
| →   | **bāvīsati**, **dvāvīsati**     |      |

|     | cha + vīsati                    | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | cha + (v→b)īsati                | 370 (§404) |
| →   | cha + (b)bīsati                 | 40 (§28)  |
| →   | **chabbīsati**                  |      |

:::

> Dasa ca dasa ca dasa cāti ‘‘dasa dasa dasā’’ti vattabbe ekasese kate ‘‘gaṇane dasassā’’tiādinā tiīsa mādesā, ‘‘kvacā’’dinā rassattaṃ, niggahītāgamo ca, sesaṃ vīsatisamaṃ. Tiṃsati, tiṃsaṃ, tiṃsa vassāni, niggahītalopo, tiṃsaṃ, tiṃsāya iccādi. Ekatiṃsati, ekatiṃsaṃ vā, bāttiṃsaṃ, dvattiṃsaṃ, tettiṃsaṃ iccādi.

`dasa ca dasa ca dasa ca` ("ten and ten and ten"): when `dasa dasa dasa` would have to be said, one alone is left, and the replacements `ti` and `īsaṃ` are made by [§389 (413): Gaṇane dasassa dviticatupañcachasattaaṭṭhanavakānaṃ vī ti cattāra paññā cha sattāsanavā yosu, yonañcīsamāsaṃ ṭhi ri tī tuti](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m389), the shortening by [§403 (354): Kvacādimajjhuttarānaṃ dīgharassā paccayesu ca](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m403), and a niggahīta is inserted; the rest is the same as for `vīsati`: `tiṃsati`, `tiṃsaṃ` ("thirty"); `tiṃsa vassāni` ("thirty years"), with the niggahīta elided; `tiṃsaṃ`, `tiṃsāya` ("by thirty"), and so on. `ekatiṃsati`, or `ekatiṃsaṃ` ("thirty-one"); `bāttiṃsaṃ`, `dvattiṃsaṃ` ("thirty-two"), `tettiṃsaṃ` ("thirty-three"), and so on.

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | dasa + dasa + dasa (①⨂)         | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | (dasa dasa dasa → dasa)         | 68 (§388) |
| →   | (dasa→ti)                       | 413 (§389) |
| →   | ti + yo                         | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | ti + (yo→īsaṃ)                  | 413 (§389) |
| →   | t + (~~i~~ + īsaṃ)              | 13 (§12)  |
| →   | t(ī→i)saṃ                       | 354 (§403) |
| →   | ti + (ṃ) + saṃ                  | 57 (§37)  |
| →   | **tiṃsaṃ**                      |      |

|     | tiṃsaṃ                          | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | tiṃsaṃ + (ti)                   | 414 (§378) |
| →   | tiṃsa + ~~ṃ~~ + ti              | 54 (§39)  |
| →   | **tiṃsati**                     |      |

:::

:::tip[Examples]

> `tiṃsa vassāni tiṭṭhati` \
> [(32Kv 1.2.7: Cittaṭṭhitikathā)](https://tipitaka2500.github.io/tipitaka/32Kv/1/1.2/1.2.7#989) \
⚧️②⨂(ti + dasa) 🚻②⨂(vassa) ▶️🤟⨀(tiṭṭhati) \
thirty | years | it lasts

:::

> Catudasatthavācakassa katekasesassa da sassa cattāra, yo vacanassa īsaṃ, cattālīsaṃ, ‘‘la darāna’’nti rassa la ttaṃ, tālīsaṃ vā. Cattālīsatimo. Ekacattālīsaṃ, dvācattālīsaṃ, dvicattālīsaṃ, tecattālīsaṃ, ticattālīsaṃ iccādi.

For the `dasa` that expresses four tens, of which one alone has been left, `cattāra`, and for the inflection `yo`, `īsaṃ`: `cattālīsaṃ` ("forty"), with the `r` changed to `l` by [§379 (258): La darānaṃ](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m379); or `tālīsaṃ`. `cattālīsatimo` ("the fortieth"). `ekacattālīsaṃ` ("forty-one"); `dvācattālīsaṃ`, `dvicattālīsaṃ` ("forty-two"); `tecattālīsaṃ`, `ticattālīsaṃ` ("forty-three"); and so on.

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | dasa ×4 (①⨂)                    | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | (dasa … dasa → dasa)            | 68 (§388) |
| →   | (dasa→cattāra)                  | 413 (§389) |
| →   | cattāra + yo                    | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | cattāra + (yo→īsaṃ)             | 413 (§389) |
| →   | cattār + (~~a~~ + īsaṃ)         | 13 (§12)  |
| →   | cattā + (r→l)īsaṃ               | 258 (§379) (optional) |
| →   | **cattālīsaṃ**, **cattārīsaṃ**  |      |

|     | cattālīsaṃ                      | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ~~ca~~ttālīsaṃ                  | 256 (§390) |
| →   | **tālīsaṃ**                     |      |

:::

> Pañcadasatthavācakassa dasa ssa paññā, yo vacanassa āsa ñca. Paññāsaṃ, ‘‘tesu vuddhi lopā’’dinā paṇṇā deso, paṇṇāsaṃ vā. Ekapaññāsaṃ, dvepaññāsaṃ, dvipaññāsaṃ.

For the `dasa` that expresses five tens, `paññā`, and for the inflection `yo`, `āsaṃ`: `paññāsaṃ` ("fifty"); by [§404 (370): Tesu vuddhilopāgamavikāraviparītādesā ca](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m404) `paṇṇā` is the replacement: or `paṇṇāsaṃ`. `ekapaññāsaṃ` ("fifty-one"); `dvepaññāsaṃ`, `dvipaññāsaṃ` ("fifty-two").

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | dasa ×5 (①⨂)                    | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | (dasa … dasa → dasa)            | 68 (§388) |
| →   | (dasa→paññā)                    | 413 (§389) |
| →   | paññā + yo                      | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | paññā + (yo→āsaṃ)               | 413 (§389) |
| →   | paññ + (~~ā~~ + āsaṃ)           | 13 (§12)  |
| →   | (paññ→paṇṇ)āsaṃ                 | 370 (§404) (optional) |
| →   | **paññāsaṃ**, **paṇṇāsaṃ**      |      |

:::

> Chadasatthavācakassa dasa ssa cha, yo vacanassa ṭhi ādeso, ‘‘sa chassa vā’’ti sa kārādeso, saṭṭhi, dvāsaṭṭhi, dvesaṭṭhi, dvisaṭṭhi, tesaṭṭhi, tisaṭṭhi.

For the `dasa` that expresses six tens, `cha`, and for the inflection `yo` the replacement `ṭhi`; by [408 (§374): Sa chassa vā](/rupasiddhi/5-taddhitakanda#r408) `cha` is replaced by the letter `sa`: `saṭṭhi` ("sixty"); `dvāsaṭṭhi`, `dvesaṭṭhi`, `dvisaṭṭhi` ("sixty-two"); `tesaṭṭhi`, `tisaṭṭhi` ("sixty-three").

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | dasa ×6 (①⨂)                    | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | (dasa … dasa → dasa)            | 68 (§388) |
| →   | (dasa→cha)                      | 413 (§389) |
| →   | cha + yo                        | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | cha + (yo→ṭhi)                  | 413 (§389) |
| →   | (cha→sa) + ṭhi                  | 408 (§374) |
| →   | sa + (ṭ)ṭhi                     | 42 (§29)  |
| →   | saṭṭhi + si                     | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | saṭṭhi + ~~si~~                 | 74 (§220) |
| →   | **saṭṭhi**                      |      |

:::

> Sattadasatthavācakassa dasa ssa satta, yo vacanassa ri, ti ca. Sattari, sattati, dvāsattari, dvāsattati, dvisattari, disattati, tesattati, tisattati iccādi.

For the `dasa` that expresses seven tens, `satta`, and for the inflection `yo`, `ri` and `ti`: `sattari`, `sattati` ("seventy"); `dvāsattari`, `dvāsattati`, `dvisattari`, `disattati` ("seventy-two"); `tesattati`, `tisattati` ("seventy-three"); and so on.

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | dasa ×7 (①⨂)                    | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | (dasa … dasa → dasa)            | 68 (§388) |
| →   | (dasa→satta)                    | 413 (§389) |
| →   | satta + yo                      | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | satta + (yo→ri, ti)             | 413 (§389) |
| →   | **sattari**, **sattati**        |      |

:::

> Aṭṭhadasatthavācakassa dasa ssa asa, yo vacanassa īti ādeso ca. Asīti, ekāsīti, dveasīti, teasīti, caturāsīti, ‘‘kvacā’’dinā dīgho.

For the `dasa` that expresses eight tens, `asa`, and for the inflection `yo` the replacement `īti`: `asīti` ("eighty"); `ekāsīti` ("eighty-one"), `dveasīti` ("eighty-two"), `teasīti` ("eighty-three"), `caturāsīti` ("eighty-four"), with lengthening by [§403 (354): Kvacādimajjhuttarānaṃ dīgharassā paccayesu ca](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m403).

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | dasa ×8 (①⨂)                    | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | (dasa … dasa → dasa)            | 68 (§388) |
| →   | (dasa→asa)                      | 413 (§389) |
| →   | asa + yo                        | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | asa + (yo→īti)                  | 413 (§389) |
| →   | as + (~~a~~ + īti)              | 13 (§12)  |
| →   | **asīti**                       |      |

|     | catu + asīti                    | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | catu + (r) + asīti              | 34 (§35)  |
| →   | catur + (a→ā)sīti               | 354 (§403) |
| →   | **caturāsīti**                  |      |

:::

> Navadasatthavācakassa dasa ssa nava, yo vacanassa uti ca, navuti, dvānavuti, dvenavuti, dvinavuti, tenavuti, tinavuti, catunavuti, channavutiyā, channavutīnaṃ pāsaṇḍānaṃ.

For the `dasa` that expresses nine tens, `nava`, and for the inflection `yo`, `uti`: `navuti` ("ninety"); `dvānavuti`, `dvenavuti`, `dvinavuti` ("ninety-two"); `tenavuti`, `tinavuti` ("ninety-three"); `catunavuti` ("ninety-four"); `channavutiyā` ("by ninety-six"); `channavutīnaṃ pāsaṇḍānaṃ` ("of the ninety-six sects").

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | dasa ×9 (①⨂)                    | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | (dasa … dasa → dasa)            | 68 (§388) |
| →   | (dasa→nava)                     | 413 (§389) |
| →   | nava + yo                       | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | nava + (yo→uti)                 | 413 (§389) |
| →   | nav + (~~a~~ + uti)             | 13 (§12)  |
| →   | **navuti**                      |      |

|     | cha + navuti                    | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | cha + (n)navuti                 | 40 (§28)  |
| →   | **channavuti**                  |      |

:::

> ‘‘Gaṇane, dasassā’’ti ca vattate.

“`gaṇane`” and “`dasassa`” also continue.

---

<a name="r415"></a>

### 415 (§393): Dasadasakaṃ sataṃ dasakānaṃ sataṃ sahassañca yomhi (ten tens is `sataṃ`, a hundred tens `sahassaṃ`, before `yo`) :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§393 (415): Dasadasakaṃ sataṃ dasakānaṃ sataṃ sahassañca yomhi" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m393" description="ten tens is `sataṃ`, a hundred tens `sahassaṃ`, before `yo`" />
:::

> Gaṇane pariyāpannassa dasadasakatthavācakassa dasa saddassa sataṃ hoti, satadasakatthavācakassa dasa ssa sahassaṃ hoti yo mhi. Iminā nipātanena yo lopo, taddhitattā puna nāmabyapadese syā dyuppatti, niggahītassa lopo, ‘‘si’’nti a mādeso, yojanānaṃ sataṃ, sahassaṃ. Sataṃ napuṃsakamekavacanantañca, tathā sahassaṃ, vaggabhede sabbattha bahuvacanampi bhavati. Dve vīsatiyo. Evaṃ tiṃsādīsupi, dve satāni, bahūni satāni, dve sahassāni, bahūni sahassāni.

The word `dasa` that is included in counting and expresses ten tens becomes `sataṃ`, and the `dasa` that expresses a hundred tens becomes `sahassaṃ`, when `yo` follows. By this direct statement (`nipātana`) the `yo` is elided; since the word is a `taddhita`, it is treated as a noun again and the inflections `si` and the rest arise; the niggahīta is elided, and `si` is replaced by `aṃ` by [§219 (195): Siṃ](/kaccayana/2-namakappa/4-catutthakanda#m219): `yojanānaṃ sataṃ` ("a hundred leagues"), `sahassaṃ` ("a thousand"). `sataṃ` is neuter and singular, and so is `sahassaṃ`; when groups are distinguished, the plural occurs everywhere too: `dve vīsatiyo` ("two twenties"). In the same way with `tiṃsa` and the rest too: `dve satāni` ("two hundreds"), `bahūni satāni` ("many hundreds"), `dve sahassāni` ("two thousands"), `bahūni sahassāni` ("many thousands").

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | dasa ×10 + yo (①⨂)              | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | (dasa … dasa → dasa) + yo       | 68 (§388) |
| →   | (dasa + yo → sataṃ)             | 415 (§393) |
| →   | sata + ~~ṃ~~ (🚻①⨀)             |      |
| →   | sata + si                       | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | sata + (si→aṃ)                  | 195 (§219) |
| →   | **sataṃ**                       |      |

|     | sata + yo (🚻①⨂)                | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | sata + (yo→ni)                  | 199 (§217) |
| →   | sat(a→ā)ni                      | 147 (§88)  |
| →   | **satāni**                      |      |

:::

:::tip[Examples]

> `yojanānaṃ sataṃ pañca` \
> [(19Pv 4.3: Nandakapetavatthu)](https://tipitaka2500.github.io/tipitaka/19Pv/4/4.3#735) \
🚻⑥⨂(yojana) 🚻①⨀(dasa ×10) ⚧️①⨂(pañca) \
of leagues | hundred | five

:::

> Satassa dvikanti atthe chaṭṭhītappurisaṃ katvā ‘‘sataṃ dvika’’nti vattabbe ‘‘dvikādīnaṃ taduttarapadānañca nipaccante’’ti vuttiyaṃ vacanato iminā nipātanena uttarapadassa pubbanipāto, ka kāralopo ca hoti. Dvisataṃ. Evaṃ satassa tikaṃ tisataṃ, tathā catusataṃ, pañcasataṃ, chasataṃ, sattasataṃ, aṭṭhasataṃ, navasataṃ, dasasataṃ sahassaṃ hoti. Atha vā dve satāni dvisatanti digusamāso. Evaṃ tisataṃ, catusataṃ iccādi.

Having made a `tappurisa` of the sixth form ⑥ in the sense `satassa dvikaṃ` ("a pair of hundred"), when `sataṃ dvikaṃ` would have to be said, then, because the vutti says "`dvika` and the rest, together with the words that follow them, are laid down", by this direct statement (`nipātana`) the later member is placed first and the `ka` is elided: `dvisataṃ` ("two hundred"). In the same way `satassa tikaṃ` ("a triad of hundred"), `tisataṃ` ("three hundred"); likewise `catusataṃ` ("four hundred"), `pañcasataṃ` ("five hundred"), `chasataṃ` ("six hundred"), `sattasataṃ` ("seven hundred"), `aṭṭhasataṃ` ("eight hundred"), `navasataṃ` ("nine hundred"), and `dasasataṃ` ("ten hundred") is `sahassaṃ` ("a thousand"). Or else `dve satāni` ("two hundreds"), `dvisataṃ`, a `digu` compound; in the same way `tisataṃ`, `catusataṃ`, and so on.

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | sata + sa (⑥⨀) + dvika (tappurisa) | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | sata + ~~sa~~ + dvika + ~~si~~  | 332 (§317) |
| →   | (sata dvika → dvi sata)         | 415 (§393) |
| →   | dvi + sata + (~~ka~~)           | 415 (§393) |
| →   | dvisata + si                    | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | dvisata + (si→aṃ)               | 195 (§219) |
| →   | **dvisataṃ**                    |      |

|     | dvi + sata (digu)               | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | dvi + ~~yo~~ + sata + ~~yo~~    | 332 (§317) |
| →   | dvisata + si                    | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | dvisata + (si→aṃ)               | 349 (§321), 195 (§219) |
| →   | **dvisataṃ**                    |      |

:::

---

<a name="r416"></a>

### 416 (§394): Yāva taduttari dasaguṇitañca (and beyond these, each next number by multiplying by ten) :badge[vidhyaṅga]{variant=tip}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§394 (416): Yāva taduttari dasaguṇitañca" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m394" description="and beyond these, each next number by multiplying by ten" />
:::

> Yāva tāsaṃ saṅkhyānamuttari, tāva dasaguṇitañca kātabbaṃ, ettha da kāro sandhijo. Yathā – dasassa gaṇanassa dasaguṇitaṃ sataṃ hoti, satassa dasaguṇitaṃ sahassaṃ, sahassassa dasaguṇitaṃ dasasahassaṃ, idaṃ nahuta ntipi vuccati, dasasahassassa dasaguṇitaṃ satasahassaṃ, taṃ lakkha ntipi vuccati, satasahassassa dasaguṇitaṃ dasasatasahassaṃ.

As far as the numbers go beyond those numerals, so far is each also to be made by multiplying by ten; here the letter `d` is born of sandhi. For example: the number ten multiplied by ten is a hundred (`sataṃ`); a hundred multiplied by ten is a thousand (`sahassaṃ`); a thousand multiplied by ten is ten thousand (`dasasahassaṃ`), which is also called `nahutaṃ`; ten thousand multiplied by ten is a hundred thousand (`satasahassaṃ`), which is also called `lakkhaṃ`; a hundred thousand multiplied by ten is ten hundred thousand (`dasasatasahassaṃ`).

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | taṃ + uttari                    | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ta + ~~ṃ~~ + uttari             | 53 (§38)  |
| →   | ta + (d) + uttari               | 34 (§35)  |
| →   | **taduttari**                   |      |

:::

> ‘‘Yadanupapannā nipātanā sijjhantī’’ti vattate.

[423 (§391): Yadanupapannā nipātanā sijjhanti](/rupasiddhi/5-taddhitakanda#r423) continues.

---

<a name="r417"></a>

### 417 (§395): Sakanāmehi ([the higher numbers are formed] by their own names) :badge[vidhyaṅga]{variant=tip}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§395 (417): Sakanāmehi" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m395" description="[the higher numbers are formed] by their own names" />
:::

> Yāsaṃ pana saṅkhyānaṃ aniddiṭṭhanāmadheyyānaṃ yāni rūpāni, tāni sakehi nāmehi nipaccante. Satasahassānaṃ sataṃ koṭi, itthiliṅgā, ekavacanantā ca, vaggabhede bahuvacanañca bhavati, koṭisatasahassānaṃ sataṃ pakoṭi, pakoṭisatasahassānaṃ sataṃ koṭippakoṭi. Evaṃ nahutaṃ, ninnahutaṃ, akkhobhinī, bindu, abbudaṃ, nirabbudaṃ, ahahaṃ, ababaṃ, aṭaṭaṃ, sogandhikaṃ, uppalaṃ, kumudaṃ, puṇḍarīkaṃ, padumaṃ, kathānaṃ, mahākathānaṃ, asaṅkhyeyyanti.

But the forms of those numbers whose names have not been stated are laid down by their own names. A hundred hundred-thousands is a `koṭi` ("ten million", 10⁷), feminine and singular, and plural when groups are distinguished; a hundred hundred-thousand `koṭi` is a `pakoṭi` (10¹⁴); a hundred hundred-thousand `pakoṭi` is a `koṭippakoṭi` (10²¹). In the same way `nahutaṃ` (10²⁸), `ninnahutaṃ` (10³⁵), `akkhobhinī` (10⁴²), `bindu` (10⁴⁹), `abbudaṃ` (10⁵⁶), `nirabbudaṃ` (10⁶³), `ahahaṃ` (10⁷⁰), `ababaṃ` (10⁷⁷), `aṭaṭaṃ` (10⁸⁴), `sogandhikaṃ` (10⁹¹), `uppalaṃ` (10⁹⁸), `kumudaṃ` (10¹⁰⁵), `puṇḍarīkaṃ` (10¹¹²), `padumaṃ` (10¹¹⁹), `kathānaṃ` (10¹²⁶), `mahākathānaṃ` (10¹³³) and `asaṅkhyeyyaṃ` ("the incalculable", 10¹⁴⁰).

> Iccevaṃ ṭhānato ṭhānaṃ, satalakkhaguṇaṃ mataṃ; \
> Koṭippabhutinaṃ vīsa-saṅkhyānañca yathākkamaṃ.

Thus, from place to place, each is held to be a hundred `lakkha` times the one before, \
for the twenty numbers beginning with `koṭi`, in their order.

> ‘‘Dve parimāṇāni etassā’’ti viggahe –

In the analysis `dve parimāṇāni etassa` ("its measures are two") —

---

<a name="r418"></a>

### 418 (§392): Dvādito konekatthe ca (`ka` after `dvi` etc. in the sense of a group; and by `nipātana`) :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§392 (418): Dvādito ko’nekattheca" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m392" description="`ka` after `dvi` etc. in the sense of a group; and by `nipātana`" />
:::

> Dvi iccevamādito gaṇanato ka ppaccayo hoti anekatthe. Dviko rāsi dvikaṃ. Evaṃ tikaṃ, catukkaṃ, pañcakaṃ, chakkaṃ, sattakaṃ, aṭṭhakaṃ, navakaṃ, dasakaṃ, paṇṇāsakaṃ, satakaṃ, sahassakaṃ iccādi.

After the numerals `dvi` and the rest, the suffix `ka` is used in the sense of more than one (`anekattha`): `dviko rāsi` ("a heap of two"), `dvikaṃ` ("a pair"). In the same way `tikaṃ` ("a group of three"), `catukkaṃ` ("of four"), `pañcakaṃ` ("of five"), `chakkaṃ` ("of six"), `sattakaṃ` ("of seven"), `aṭṭhakaṃ` ("of eight"), `navakaṃ` ("of nine"), `dasakaṃ` ("of ten"), `paṇṇāsakaṃ` ("of fifty"), `satakaṃ` ("of a hundred"), `sahassakaṃ` ("of a thousand"), and so on.

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | dvi + yo (①⨂) + ka              | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | dvi + ~~yo~~ + ka               | 332 (§317) |
| →   | dvika + si                      | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | dvika + (si→aṃ)                 | 195 (§219) |
| →   | **dvikaṃ**                      |      |

|     | catu + yo (①⨂) + ka             | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | catu + ~~yo~~ + ka              | 332 (§317) |
| →   | catu + (k)ka                    | 40 (§28)  |
| →   | catukka + si                    | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | catukka + (si→aṃ)               | 195 (§219) |
| →   | **catukkaṃ**                    |      |

:::

---

> _Saṅkhyātaddhitaṃ._

[Thus ends] the `saṅkhyātaddhita`, the numeral suffixes.

---

## Abyayataddhita (Adverbial suffixes)

> ‘‘Ekasmiṃ vāre bhuñjati, dvivāre bhuñjatī’’ti viggahe –

In the analysis `ekasmiṃ vāre bhuñjati` ("he eats on one occasion"), `dvivāre bhuñjati` ("he eats on two occasions") —

---

<a name="r419"></a>

### 419 (§646): Ekādito sakissa kkhattuṃ (`kkhattuṃ` for `sakiṃ` after `eka` and the other numerals: "once", "twice") :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§646 (419): Ekādito sakissa kkhattuṃ" href="/kaccayana/8-unadikappa#m646" description="`kkhattuṃ` for `sakiṃ` after `eka` and the other numerals: 'once', 'twice'" />
:::

> Ekadviti iccevamādito gaṇanato sakissa ṭhāne vāratthe kkhattuṃ paccayo hoti. Ekakkhattuṃ, dvikkhattuṃ bhuñjati, ‘‘sabbāsamāvuso’’tiādinā si lopo. Evaṃ tikkhattuṃ, catukkhattuṃ, pañcakkhattuṃ, chakkhattuṃ, sattakkhattuṃ, aṭṭhakkhattuṃ, navakkhattuṃ, dasakkhattuṃ, satakkhattuṃ, sahassakkhattuṃ, bahukkhattuṃ, katikkhattuṃ.

After the numerals `eka`, `dvi`, `ti` and the rest, in place of `sakiṃ` ("once"), the suffix `kkhattuṃ` is used in the sense of occasions: `ekakkhattuṃ` ("once"), `dvikkhattuṃ bhuñjati` ("he eats twice"); the `si` is elided by [§221 (282): Sabbāsamāvuso pasagganipātādīhi ca](/kaccayana/2-namakappa/4-catutthakanda#m221). In the same way `tikkhattuṃ` ("three times"), `catukkhattuṃ` ("four times"), `pañcakkhattuṃ` ("five times"), `chakkhattuṃ` ("six times"), `sattakkhattuṃ` ("seven times"), `aṭṭhakkhattuṃ` ("eight times"), `navakkhattuṃ` ("nine times"), `dasakkhattuṃ` ("ten times"), `satakkhattuṃ` ("a hundred times"), `sahassakkhattuṃ` ("a thousand times"), `bahukkhattuṃ` ("many times"), `katikkhattuṃ` ("how many times?").

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | eka + smiṃ (⑦⨀) + kkhattuṃ      | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | eka + ~~smiṃ~~ + kkhattuṃ       | 332 (§317) |
| →   | ekakkhattuṃ + si                | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | ekakkhattuṃ + ~~si~~            | 282 (§221) |
| →   | **ekakkhattuṃ**                 |      |

|     | dvi + smiṃ (⑦⨀) + kkhattuṃ      | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | dvi + ~~smiṃ~~ + kkhattuṃ       | 332 (§317) |
| →   | dvikkhattuṃ + si                | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | dvikkhattuṃ + ~~si~~            | 282 (§221) |
| →   | **dvikkhattuṃ**                 |      |

:::

:::tip[Examples]

> `sahassakkhattuṃ attānaṃ abhinimminitvā` \
> [(12S1 6.1.6: Brahmalokasutta)](https://tipitaka2500.github.io/tipitaka/12S1/6/6.1/6.1.6#1065) \
🔼(sahassa + kkhattuṃ) 🚹②⨀(atta) 🔽(abhinimminitvā) \
a thousand times | himself | having created

:::

> ‘‘Ekena vibhāgenā’’ti viggahe –

In the analysis `ekena vibhāgena` ("by one division") —

> Maṇḍūkagatiyā saṅkhyā ggahaṇamanuvattate.

The mention of the numeral (`saṅkhyā`) continues selectively ([`maṇḍūkagati`](/introduction#types-of-suttas)).

---

<a name="r420"></a>

### 420 (§397): Vibhāge dhā ca (`dhā` in the sense of division; and `so`) :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§397 (420): Vibhāge dhā ca" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m397" description="`dhā` in the sense of division; and `so`" />
:::

:::note[Analysis]

The mention of the numeral (`saṅkhyā`) that continues selectively into this rule is that of [406 (§373): Saṅkhyāpūraṇe mo](/rupasiddhi/5-taddhitakanda#r406), over the rules between.

:::

> Vibhāgatthe ekādisaṅkhyāto dhā paccayo hoti. Ca saddena eka dvito jjha ca, suttā dito so ca. Ekadhā. Dvīhi vibhāgehi dvidhā, dudhā vā, dvedhā. Tīhi vibhāgehi tidhā, tedhā vā, ‘‘tesu vuddhī’’tiādinā ikārassekāro. Evaṃ catudhā, pañcadhā, chadhā, sattadhā, aṭṭhadhā, navadhā, dasadhā, satadhā, sahassadhā, katidhā, bahudhā.

In the sense of division (`vibhāga`), the suffix `dhā` is used after the numerals `eka` and the rest; by the word `ca`, `jjha` too after `eka` and `dvi`, and `so` after `sutta` and the like. `ekadhā` ("in one way"). `dvīhi vibhāgehi` ("by two divisions"): `dvidhā`, or `dudhā`, `dvedhā` ("in two ways"). `tīhi vibhāgehi` ("by three divisions"): `tidhā`, or `tedhā` ("in three ways"), the `i` becoming `e` by [§404 (370): Tesu vuddhilopāgamavikāraviparītādesā ca](/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m404). In the same way `catudhā` ("in four ways"), `pañcadhā` ("in five"), `chadhā` ("in six"), `sattadhā` ("in seven"), `aṭṭhadhā` ("in eight"), `navadhā` ("in nine"), `dasadhā` ("in ten"), `satadhā` ("in a hundred"), `sahassadhā` ("in a thousand ways"), `katidhā` ("in how many ways?"), `bahudhā` ("in many ways").

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | eka + nā (③⨀) + dhā             | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | eka + ~~nā~~ + dhā              | 332 (§317) |
| →   | ekadhā + si                     | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | ekadhā + ~~si~~                 | 282 (§221) |
| →   | **ekadhā**                      |      |

|     | dvi + hi (③⨂) + dhā             | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | dvi + ~~hi~~ + dhā              | 332 (§317) |
| →   | dvidhā + si                     | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | dvidhā + ~~si~~                 | 282 (§221) |
| →   | **dvidhā**, **dudhā**, **dvedhā** |      |

|     | ti + hi (③⨂) + dhā              | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ti + ~~hi~~ + dhā               | 332 (§317) |
| →   | t(i→e)dhā                       | 370 (§404) (optional) |
| →   | tedhā + si                      | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | tedhā + ~~si~~                  | 282 (§221) |
| →   | **tedhā**, **tidhā**            |      |

:::

> Jjhappaccaye ekadhā karotīti ekajjhaṃ. Evaṃ dvejjhaṃ.

With the suffix `jjha`: `ekadhā karoti` ("he makes into one"), `ekajjhaṃ` ("together"). In the same way `dvejjhaṃ` ("in two").

> So paccaye suttena vibhāgena suttaso. Evaṃ byañjanaso, padaso, atthaso, bahuso, sabbākārena sabbaso, upāyaso, hetuso, ṭhānaso, yoniso.

With the suffix `so`: `suttena vibhāgena` ("by division into suttas"), `suttaso` ("sutta by sutta"). In the same way `byañjanaso` ("letter by letter"), `padaso` ("word by word"), `atthaso` ("according to the sense"), `bahuso` ("frequently"); `sabbākārena` ("in every way"), `sabbaso`; `upāyaso` ("by way of means"), `hetuso` ("by cause"), `ṭhānaso` ("on the spot"), `yoniso` ("thoroughly").

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | eka + jjha                      | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ekajjha + si                    | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | ekajjha + (si→aṃ)               | 195 (§219) |
| →   | **ekajjhaṃ**                    |      |

|     | sutta + nā (③⨀) + so            | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | sutta + ~~nā~~ + so             | 332 (§317) |
| →   | suttaso + si                    | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | suttaso + ~~si~~                | 282 (§221) |
| →   | **suttaso**                     |      |

:::

<a name="r421"></a>

### 421 (§398): Sabbanāmehi pakāravacane tu thā (`thā` after pronouns in the sense of manner; and `thattā`) :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§398 (421): Sabbanāmehi pakāravacane tu thā" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m398" description="`thā` after pronouns in the sense of manner; and `thattā`" />
:::

> Sabbanāmehi pakāravacanatthe thā paccayo hoti, tu saddena thattā paccayo ca. Sāmaññassa bhedako viseso pakāro, tassābhidhāneti attho, so pakāro tathā, taṃ pakāraṃ tathā, tena pakārena tathā, yena pakārena yathā. Evaṃ sabbathā, aññathā, itarathā, ubhayathā, thattā paccaye tena pakārena tathattā. Evaṃ yathattā, aññathattā.

After pronouns, the suffix `thā` is used in the sense of the expression of manner (`pakāravacana`), and by the word `tu` the suffix `thattā` as well. The manner (`pakāra`) is the particular that distinguishes the general; the meaning is "in the expression of that". `so pakāro` ("that manner"), `tathā` ("thus"); `taṃ pakāraṃ` ("that manner", ②), `tathā`; `tena pakārena` ("by that manner"), `tathā`; `yena pakārena` ("by which manner"), `yathā` ("as"). In the same way `sabbathā` ("in every way"), `aññathā` ("otherwise"), `itarathā` ("in another way"), `ubhayathā` ("in both ways"). With the suffix `thattā`: `tena pakārena`, `tathattā` ("in that manner"). In the same way `yathattā` ("in which manner"), `aññathattā` ("otherwise").

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | ta + si (①⨀) + thā              | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ta + ~~si~~ + thā               | 332 (§317) |
| →   | tathā + si                      | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | tathā + ~~si~~                  | 282 (§221) |
| →   | **tathā**                       |      |

|     | ta + nā (③⨀) + thattā           | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ta + ~~nā~~ + thattā            | 332 (§317) |
| →   | tathattā + si                   | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | tathattā + ~~si~~               | 282 (§221) |
| →   | **tathattā**                    |      |

:::

> Ko pakāroti atthe –

In the sense `ko pakāro` ("what manner?") —

---

<a name="r422"></a>

### 422 (§399): Kimimehi thaṃ (`thaṃ` after `kiṃ` and `ima` in the sense of manner) :badge[apavada]{variant=success}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§399 (422): Kimimehi thaṃ" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m399" description="`thaṃ` after `kiṃ` and `ima` in the sense of manner" />
:::

> Kiṃima iccetehi thaṃ paccayo hoti pakāravacanatthe. ‘‘Kissa ka ve cā’’ti ettha ca saddena ki ssa kā deso. Kathaṃ, kaṃ pakāraṃ kathaṃ, kena pakārena kathaṃ, ayaṃ pakāro itthaṃ, imaṃ pakāraṃ itthaṃ. Anena pakārena itthaṃ, ‘‘imassi thaṃ dāniha to dhesu cā’’ti ima saddassa i kāro, dvittaṃ. Ettha hi kkhattuṃ ādithaṃ pariyosānappaccayantānaṃ abyayataddhitattā nāmabyapadesaṃ katvā vibhattimhi kate ‘‘sabbāsamāvuso’’tiādinā vibhattilopo, ‘‘kvaci to pañcamyatthe’’tiādinā vuttato ādippaccayantā ca idheva a byayataddhite saṅgayhanti.

After `kiṃ` and `ima`, the suffix `thaṃ` is used in the sense of the expression of manner. By the word `ca` in [§227 (270): Kissa ka ve ca](/kaccayana/2-namakappa/4-catutthakanda#m227), `kiṃ` is replaced by `ka`: `kathaṃ` ("how?"); `kaṃ pakāraṃ` ("what manner?", ②), `kathaṃ`; `kena pakārena` ("by what manner?"), `kathaṃ`. `ayaṃ pakāro` ("this manner"), `itthaṃ` ("thus"); `imaṃ pakāraṃ`, `itthaṃ`; `anena pakārena` ("by this manner"), `itthaṃ`; by [§234 (265): Imassi thaṃ dāni ha to dhesu ca](/kaccayana/2-namakappa/4-catutthakanda#m234) the word `ima` becomes the letter `i`, and there is doubling. Here, indeed, the words ending in the suffixes from `kkhattuṃ` to `thaṃ` are uninflected derivatives (`abyayataddhita`), so once they have been treated as nouns and an inflection has been added, that inflection is elided by [§221 (282): Sabbāsamāvuso pasagganipātādīhi ca](/kaccayana/2-namakappa/4-catutthakanda#m221); and the words ending in `to` and the other suffixes taught from [§248 (260): Kvaci to pañcamyatthe](/kaccayana/2-namakappa/5-pancamakanda#m248) onward are also included right here, among the uninflected derivatives.

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | kiṃ + si (①⨀) + thaṃ            | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | kiṃ + ~~si~~ + thaṃ             | 332 (§317) |
| →   | (kiṃ→ka) + thaṃ                 | 270 (§227) |
| →   | kathaṃ + si                     | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | kathaṃ + ~~si~~                 | 282 (§221) |
| →   | **kathaṃ**                      |      |

|     | ima + si (①⨀) + thaṃ            | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ima + ~~si~~ + thaṃ             | 332 (§317) |
| →   | (ima→i) + thaṃ                  | 265 (§234) |
| →   | i + (thaṃ→tthaṃ)                | 42 (§29)  |
| →   | itthaṃ + si                     | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | itthaṃ + ~~si~~                 | 282 (§221) |
| →   | **itthaṃ**                      |      |

:::

---

<a name="r423"></a>

### 423 (§391): Yadanupapannā nipātanā sijjhanti (forms not reached by a rule are established by direct statement) :badge[vidhyaṅga]{variant=tip}

:::tip[Kaccāyana]
<LinkCard title="§391 (423): Yadanupapannā nipātanā sijjhanti" href="/kaccayana/5-taddhitakappa#m391" description="forms not reached by a rule are established by direct statement" />
:::

> Ye saddā lakkhaṇena anupapannā a niddiṭṭhalakkhaṇā akkharādito, nāmopasagganipātato vā samāsataddhitādito vā, te nipātanā sijjhanti.

Those words which are not accounted for by a rule (`lakkhaṇa`), for which no rule has been stated — whether from the letters and the rest, from nouns, prefixes and particles, or from compounds, `taddhita` and the rest — are established by direct statement (`nipātana`).

> Taddhitato tāva –

From the `taddhita` first —

> Imasmā jja siyā kāle, samānāparato jju ca; \
> Imasaddassa’kāro ca, samānassa ca so siyā.

After `ima`, in the sense of time, let there be `jja`, and `jju` after `samāna` and `apara`; \
and let the word `ima` become the letter `a`, and `samāna` become `sa`.

> Imasmiṃ kāle, imasmiṃ divase vā ajja, samāne kāle sajju, aparasmiṃdivase aparajju. Nipātehi bhavatthe tana ppaccayo. Ajja bhavaṃ ajjatanaṃ, ajja bhavā ajjatanī, sve bhavaṃ svātanaṃ. Evaṃ purātanaṃ, hiyyo bhavaṃ hiyyattanaṃ, hiyyo bhavā hiyyattanī iccādi.

`imasmiṃ kāle` ("at this time"), or `imasmiṃ divase` ("on this day"): `ajja` ("today"). `samāne kāle` ("at the same time"): `sajju` ("at once"). `aparasmiṃ divase` ("on the following day"): `aparajju` ("the next day"). After particles, the suffix `tana` is used in the sense of being (`bhava`): `ajja bhavaṃ` ("being today"), `ajjatanaṃ` ("of today"); `ajja bhavā`, `ajjatanī` ("of today": the aorist tense); `sve bhavaṃ` ("being tomorrow"), `svātanaṃ` ("of tomorrow"). In the same way `purātanaṃ` ("of old"); `hiyyo bhavaṃ` ("being yesterday"), `hiyyattanaṃ` ("of yesterday"); `hiyyo bhavā`, `hiyyattanī` ("of yesterday": the imperfect tense); and so on.

:::tip[Derivation]

|     | ima + smiṃ (⑦⨀) + jja           | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ima + ~~smiṃ~~ + jja            | 332 (§317) |
| →   | (ima→a) + jja                   | 423 (§391) |
| →   | ajja + si                       | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | ajja + ~~si~~                   | 282 (§221) |
| →   | **ajja**                        |      |

|     | samāna + smiṃ (⑦⨀) + jju        | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | samāna + ~~smiṃ~~ + jju         | 332 (§317) |
| →   | (samāna→sa) + jju               | 423 (§391) |
| →   | sajju + si                      | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | sajju + ~~si~~                  | 282 (§221) |
| →   | **sajju**                       |      |

|     | ajja + tana                     | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ajjatana + si                   | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | ajjatana + (si→aṃ)              | 195 (§219) |
| →   | **ajjatanaṃ**                   |      |

|     | ajjatana + ī (🚺)               | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | ajjatan + (~~a~~ + ī)           | 187 (§238), 67 (§83) |
| →   | ajjatanī + si                   | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | ajjatanī + ~~si~~               | 74 (§220) |
| →   | **ajjatanī**                    |      |

|     | hiyyo + tana                    | rule |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ---- |
| →   | hiyy(o→a) + (t)tana             | 370 (§404), 40 (§28) |
| →   | hiyyattana + si                 | 334 (§601), 63 (§55) |
| →   | hiyyattana + (si→aṃ)            | 195 (§219) |
| →   | **hiyyattanaṃ**                 |      |

:::

---

> _Abyayataddhitaṃ._

[Thus ends] the `abyayataddhita`, the uninflected derivatives.

---

> Sāmaññavuttibhāvatthā-byayato taddhitaṃ tidhā; \
> Tatrādi catudhāpaccā-nekatthassatthisaṅkhyāto.

The `taddhita` is threefold: of general application, in the abstract sense, and uninflected; \
the first of these is fourfold: of descent, of many senses, of possession and of number.

---

> _Iti padarūpasiddhiyaṃ taddhitakaṇḍo pañcamo._

Thus ends the fifth section of the Padarūpasiddhi, the Taddhitakaṇḍa.

---
